Further segment-level and document-level analysis are required to assess readability, factual consistency and user-oriented adequacy.

</details>


### [39] [Know Your Source: A Public Knowledge Store for Media Background Checks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02383)
*Benjamin Nichols,Michael Schlichtkrull,Nedjma Ousidhoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 MEDIAREF，一个公开的知识库，包含 200 个媒体来源的网络文档，用于可复现、低成本的媒体背景检查（MBC）生成评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 RAG 的事实核查系统假设检索证据可靠，但现实中信息可能冲突、过时、来自不可靠或有偏见的来源。源批判推理通过媒体背景检查（MBC）评估来源可信度，但生成 MBC 依赖昂贵的专有搜索 API，限制了可复现性。

Method: 构建 MEDIAREF 知识库，提供可复现的构建与更新方法论；在 MBC 生成任务上评估主流大语言模型，并通过自动与定性评估对比 MEDIAREF 的效果。

Result: 实验表明，MEDIAREF 能够支持更高质量的 MBC 生成，在自动指标和定性分析上均优于依赖专有 API 的基线。

Conclusion: MEDIAREF 解决了 MBC 生成依赖昂贵专有 API 的问题，提供了一个可复现、低成本的公共评估资源，推动源批判推理在自动事实核查中的应用。

Abstract: LLM-based retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly used for automated fact-checking (AFC) and related tasks. By grounding LLM outputs in retrieved evidence, RAG-based systems provide transparent justifications while allowing external information to be updated independently of the underlying model. However, existing approaches often assume retrieved evidence is reliable, although real-world information may be conflicting, outdated, and can originate from unreliable or biased sources. Recent work on *source-critical reasoning* addresses this challenge through media background checks (MBCs) (Schlichtkrull, 2024), which assess the credibility of evidence sources to support downstream fact verification. However, generating MBCs relies on costly proprietary search APIs, limiting reproducibility. To mitigate this issue, we introduce MEDIAREF, a publicly available knowledge store of web-sourced documents that enables reproducible, low-cost evaluation of MBC generation across 200 media sources. We describe a reproducible methodology for constructing and updating the collection, assess widely used LLMs on the MBC generation task, and demonstrate that MEDIAREF supports higher-quality MBC generation through both automatic and qualitative evaluation.

</details>


### [40] [The Future of NLP may not be at NLP Conferences: Scholarly Migration Patterns in Natural Language Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02416)
*David Jurgens*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文分析了2010-2026年NLP研究发表趋势，发现随着大语言模型（LLM）的兴起，NLP研究的发表重心正从传统的*ACL主会转向Findings轨道和通用机器学习会议，且通用ML会议带来显著引用溢价，影响作者投稿选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型（LLM）进步是否模糊了NLP与通用机器学习（ML）的学科界限，导致NLP研究发表重心发生系统性迁移。

Method: 利用2010-2026年NLP研究发表数据，对比LLM出现前后资深作者及新作者的投稿分布变化，采用因果推断技术估计通用ML会议的引用溢价及其对会议选择的影响。

Result: 资深作者在*ACL主会份额下降19.2个百分点，Findings轨道上升14.8个百分点，通用ML会议上升8.6个百分点；新作者首发多篇NLP论文中主要发表于*ACL会议的比例从84%（2019）降至74%（2024），主要发表于通用ML会议的比例从5%升至21%；因果推断显示通用ML会议存在显著引用溢价。

Conclusion: NLP研究的发表重心正显著向通用机器学习会议迁移，这种迁移由引用溢价驱动，标志着NLP与ML学科边界的模糊化和发表生态的重塑。

Abstract: Natural Language Processing (NLP) has traditionally been published in its core disciplinary venues like ACL. However, advances in Large Language Models (LLMs) has led to a blurring of the disciplinary lines between NLP and general Machine Learning (ML), with authors regularly publishing in venues from both fields. Here, we ask whether the disciplinary center of gravity is shifting. Using NLP research published from 2010 to 2026 and studies of both established and new authors, we find that a migration is taking place. First, comparing the pre- and post-LLM eras, established authors lost 19.2pp of share at flagship *ACL main-conference tracks while gaining 14.8pp in the newer Findings tracks, and general ML venues rose 8.6pp, even when adjusting for parallel growth in the fields. Second, among newer authors who debut with at least three first-author NLP-topic papers, the share whose work appears mostly at *ACL venues fell from 84% (2019) to 74% (2024), while the share appearing mostly at general ML venues rose from 5% to 21%. Using causal inference techniques, we estimate that these general ML venues confer a significant citation premium, which influences venue selection. Together, these results point to a significant shift in where NLP research is published.

</details>


### [41] [Language Models as Measurement Apparatus for Culture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02459)
*Kent K. Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文探讨自然语言处理（NLP）中文化测量的本质，认为语言模型对文化的量化并非被动记录，而是通过模型、数据、标注、评估等装置主动构建文化现实。基于Karen Barad的“能动性切割”概念，指出装置的设计选择划定了现象与工具的边界，且语言模型已内化了被测量的文化材料。通过电视电影对话的三个案例研究及装置自身的三个审视，提出一个理论驱动、实证严谨且文化情境化的研究计划，将每个能动性切割视为方法论与伦理的双重承诺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前NLP研究广泛使用语言模型量化文化现象，但缺乏对“什么使此类测量具有独特文化性”的反思。现有工作往往假设模型客观记录文化，忽视了测量装置本身参与构建文化现实的物质-话语实践属性，以及模型预训练中已内化文化材料导致的边界纠缠问题。

Method: 采用理论驱动的分析框架，结合Karen Barad的“能动性切割”概念，通过三个层面的实证研究：1) 电视电影对话的三个案例研究（测量结构、互动、偏差）；2) 对测量装置本身的三个审视（文化标记的抹除、对历史材料的调谐、能动性工作流中的能动性）；3) 综合分析提出研究计划。

Result: 发现语言模型测量文化的装置通过实质性设计选择（模型架构、数据选择、标注规范、评估指标）划定现象与工具的边界，即“能动性切割”。这种边界从一开始就纠缠在一起，因为语言模型预训练已内化大量被测量的文化材料。案例研究显示：文化标记在处理中被抹除，模型对历史材料的调谐反映特定文化视角，能动性工作流中人类与模型能动性相互构成。

Conclusion: NLP文化研究应转向将每个能动性切割视为有意识的方法论与伦理承诺的研究计划。这要求理论驱动（引入科学技术研究等批判理论）、实证严谨（系统案例研究）、文化情境化（承认测量的情境依赖性），从而超越技术解决主义，建立负责任的文化计算实践。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly used to quantify cultural phenomena, but what makes such measurement distinctively cultural? This paper argues that NLP work on culture is a material-discursive practice: the apparatus -- model, data, annotation, evaluation -- participates in constituting the cultural reality it measures, rather than passively recording it. Drawing on Karen Barad's concept of the agential cut -- the contingent boundary between phenomenon and instrument -- I show that the apparatus's substantive design choices draw such boundaries, and that the boundary is entangled from the start because language models have already internalized much of the cultural material they measure. I illustrate this through three case studies on television and film dialogue (measuring structure, interaction, and deviation) and three examinations of the apparatus itself (erasure of cultural markers, attunement to historical material, and agency in an agentic workflow). This big picture analysis proposes a research program that is theory-driven, empirically rigorous, and culturally contingent, treating each agential cut as a conscious commitment, at once methodological and ethical.

</details>


### [42] [Will Scaling Improve Social Simulation with LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02464)
*Caleb Ziems,William Held,Su Doga Karaca,David Grusky,Tatsunori Hashimoto,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过扩展定律研究大语言模型（LLM）的计算规模与社会模拟保真度的关系，发现大多数观点建模和行为模拟任务随规模显著提升，但纵向预测、代表性不足群体及认知偏差校准等任务扩展效果有限，且微调也难以改善。结论：规模扩展能提升大多数社会模拟场景，但低资源领域和特定认知任务存在异常，改进不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM社会模拟虽具潜力，但保真度不足以广泛采用。研究旨在探究现有语言建模扩展范式是否能弥补这些差距，还是模拟保真度与通用能力正交、需要更多专门研究。

Method: 采用扩展定律方法，使用85个Qwen3架构的Transformer LLMs（在DCLM网络语料上预训练，固定计算预算10^18至10^20 FLOPs），评估三个代表性子领域：观点建模、行为模拟、纵向预测。另评估35个更大的开放权重模型（高达70B参数），从损失预测下游准确率。

Result: 1) 三个子领域均呈现强计算扩展规律；2) 大多数行为和观点模拟任务随规模快速改进，尤其英语网络语料中代表性好的人群；3) 纵向预测和代表性不足观点扩展较慢，特别是与MMLU等通用知识推理基准相关性低时；4) 行为模拟中，规模扩展无法改善模型对人类认知偏差（如风险厌恶）和启发式（如从相关任务学习关联奖励）的校准，即使微调模型在0.5B到8B参数范围内也无显著提升。

Conclusion: 规模扩展将在大多数设置下改进社会模拟，但存在异常情况，且在低资源领域改进将不可靠。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) social simulations are a promising research method, but they are not yet faithful enough to be adopted widely. In this work, we investigate whether the current scaling paradigm in language modeling is likely to close these gaps, or whether simulation fidelity is orthogonal to general capabilities and therefore deserving of more research attention. We use scaling laws to study the relationship between LLMs' compute scale, general capability benchmarks, and the fidelity of social simulation in three representative sub-domains: opinion modeling, behavioral simulation, and longitudinal forecasting. Surprisingly, we discover strong compute scaling in all three settings, using a suite of 85 transformer LLMs with the Qwen3 architecture pre-trained on the DCLM web text corpus under fixed-compute budgets from $10^{18}$ to $10^{20}$ FLOPs. Then we evaluate 35 larger and more capable open-weight models up to 70B parameters, allowing us to predict downstream accuracy from loss. This reveals that the majority of behavioral and opinion simulation tasks will rapidly improve with scale, particularly when they involve populations that are well-represented in English web corpora. Longitudinal forecasting and underrepresented opinions scale more slowly, especially when they are less correlated with general knowledge and reasoning benchmarks like MMLU. In behavior simulation, scaling fails to improve model calibration with human cognitive biases like risk aversion, as well as human heuristics like learning correlated rewards from related tasks. On these tasks, even fine-tuned models fail to noticeably scale up performance from 0.5B to 8B parameters. Taken together, we conclude that scale will improve social simulations in most settings, but outliers exist, and improvements will be less reliable in low-resource domains.

</details>


### [43] [Audio-Based Understanding of Audiobook Narration Appeal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02473)
*Shahar Elisha,Mariano Beguerisse-Díaz,Emmanouil Benetos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究首次系统性地利用计算方法将朗读质量（声学特征）、体裁、标题与有声书消费数据关联，发现声学信息对吸引力有稳健关联，可用于个性化推荐和朗读者选角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索朗读质量（如语调、语速、响度）如何影响有声书的吸引力，且这种影响可能因体裁、标题和受众而异，旨在为有声书个性化推荐和朗读者选角提供数据驱动的洞察。

Method: 从LibriVox提取声学特征（使用预训练音频模型），分析这些特征与消费数据（观看率）的关系，并考察其与体裁和标题的交互作用；进一步使用专有的更细粒度参与度指标验证发现。

Result: 尽管消费数据有限，声学信息本身与吸引力存在稳健关联，即使控制标题效应后依然显著；专有指标验证了这一发现。

Conclusion: 这是首个系统性计算研究，将朗读质量、体裁、标题与有声书消费联系起来，展示了数据驱动洞察在改进有声书个性化推荐和朗读者选角方面的潜力。

Abstract: Narration is central to the audiobook listening experience, shaping how listeners engage with and understand the content. This work explores how narration qualities shape an audiobook's appeal, noting that their effects can vary by genre, title, and audience. We extract vocal and acoustic features (e.g., tone, pace, loudness) from LibriVox using pre-trained audio models and analyse their relationship with consumption data (specifically, view-rate) and their interplay with genre and title. Despite limited consumption data, we find that acoustic information alone has a robust association with appeal, even after accounting for title effects. We further validate these findings using more nuanced proprietary engagement metrics. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic computational study linking narration qualities, genre, title, and audiobook consumption, highlighting the potential of data-driven insights to improve audiobook personalisation and narrator casting.

</details>


### [44] [Visually Grounded Self-Reflection for Vision-Language Models via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02490)
*Liyan Tang,Fangcong Yin,Greg Durrett*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为VRRL的强化学习训练框架，旨在提升大型视觉语言模型在多模态推理中的视觉接地自我反思能力，通过随机掩码轨迹前缀和缓冲回滚经验回放，显著提高了分布外图像上的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型视觉语言模型在思维链推理中展现自我反思能力，但在反思过程中往往无法正确关注视觉输入，导致难以将反馈转化为基于视觉的纠错，尤其在分布外图像上表现不佳。

Method: 提出VRRL框架，包含两个组件：1) 随机掩码训练轨迹前缀，强调从错误的中间预测中恢复，而非避免早期错误；2) 从经验回放缓冲区引入缓冲回滚，使模型接触多样化的失败状态并学会纠正。

Result: 在表格和图表的视觉定位任务以及空间导航基准上评估，与现成模型和常规微调模型在分布偏移下大幅退化相比，VRRL显著提高了平均分布外准确率，优于标准强化学习和面向反思的微调基线。

Conclusion: VRRL通过明确设计的强化学习机制有效激发了视觉接地的自我反思，解决了LVLMs在分布外场景下反思时忽视视觉输入的问题，实现了更鲁棒的多模态推理。

Abstract: Large vision-language models can reason over multimodal inputs by generating textual chains of thought (CoT). A key capability exhibited in CoT reasoning is self-reflection: revisiting earlier decisions and correcting previous errors. However, existing LVLMs often fail to properly attend to visual inputs during reflection, limiting their ability to translate feedback into grounded corrections, especially for out-of-distribution images. To address this issue, we propose a novel reinforcement learning training framework VRRL, with two components explicitly designed to elicit visually grounded self-reflection. First, we randomly mask trajectory prefixes during training to emphasize recovery from incorrect intermediate predictions rather than making early mistakes. Second, we introduce buffered roll-ins from an experience replay buffer to expose the model to diverse failure states that it must learn to correct. We evaluate our approach on visual grounding tasks involving tables and charts, as well as spatial navigation benchmarks. While off-the-shelf and conventionally fine-tuned models degrade substantially under distribution shift, our method substantially improves average out-of-distribution accuracy over standard RL and reflection-oriented fine-tuning baselines by using self-reflection effectively.

</details>


### [45] [Reasoning LLM Improves Speaker Recognition in Long-form TV Dramas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02504)
*Yuxuan Li,Lingxi Xie,Xinyue Huo,Jihao Qiu,Jiacheng Shao,Pengfei Chen,Jiannan Ge,Kaiwen Duan,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个大规模长视频剧集说话人识别基准DramaSR-532K和基于大型推理模型的DramaSR-LRM方法，显著提升了说话人归属准确性，尤其在短语句上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频剧集理解依赖说话人识别，现有方法在复杂场景和短语句上表现不足，缺乏大规模多模态基准。

Method: 构建包含532K标注对话、900+角色的DramaSR-532K基准；提出DramaSR-LRM，基于大型推理模型，通过多模态工具自主聚合听觉、语言、视觉上下文证据进行高保真归属。

Result: 实验表明DramaSR-LRM显著优于现有基线，特别是在声纹不可靠的短语句上效果显著。

Conclusion: 本工作为长视频剧集说话人识别提供了大规模基准和有效方法，推动了多模态推理在视频理解中的应用。

Abstract: Long-form TV dramas present a formidable challenge for comprehensive video understanding, where deciphering complex storyline often relies on \textbf{speaker recognition}, the task of accurately attributing each spoken utterance to its respective character. In this paper, we advance this field through two primary contributions. (1) We introduce \textbf{DramaSR-532K}, a large-scale benchmark comprising 532K annotated dialogue lines across more than 900 unique characters, necessitating the integration of auditory, linguistic, and visual cues for speaker recognition. (2) We propose \textbf{DramaSR-LRM}, a robust approach built upon a large reasoning model (LRM). DramaSR-LRM is designed to autonomously aggregate contextual evidence via multimodal tool-use, synthesizing diverse inputs to achieve high-fidelity attribution. Experimental results demonstrate that DramaSR-LRM significantly outperforms existing baselines, particularly on short utterances where acoustic biometrics are inherently unreliable. \textit{All the data and code will be made publicly available at the project page: https://www.github.com/198808xc/DramaSR-LRM.}

</details>


### [46] [LACUNA: A Testbed for Evaluating Localization Precision for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02513)
*Matteo Boglioni,Thibault Rousset,Siva Reddy,Marius Mosbach,Verna Dankers*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出LACUNA测试床，首次提供参数级定位的真实标签，用于评估大语言模型遗忘方法是否真正擦除参数中的知识，而非仅掩盖输出。实验表明现有SOTA遗忘方法在参数级定位上不精确，易受重现攻击；精确定位后，简单梯度遗忘即可实现稳健擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型会记忆训练数据中的敏感个人信息（PII），现有遗忘方法多采用“先定位后遗忘”范式，但现有基准仅在输出层面评估，无法判断遗忘是否真正从参数中擦除知识，还是仅掩盖输出。重现攻击的成功加剧了这一担忧，亟需参数级真实标签的评估基准。

Method: 构建LACUNA测试床：通过掩码持续预训练，将合成个体的PII注入到1B和7B OLMo模型的预定义参数中，获得参数级定位的真实标签。在此基础上对当前SOTA遗忘方法进行基准测试，并分析定位精度与遗忘效果、重现攻击鲁棒性的关系。

Result: 尽管现有SOTA方法在输出层面表现强劲，但参数级定位极不精确，且易受重现攻击。当定位成功时，即使简单的基于梯度的遗忘方法也能实现强擦除效果并抵御重现攻击，凸显精确定位对稳健遗忘的关键作用。

Conclusion: LACUNA填补了参数级遗忘评估的空白，揭示了当前方法的局限性，并证明精确参数定位是实现稳健遗忘的核心。发布LACUNA以补充行为评估，推动基于定位的稳健遗忘研究。

Abstract: LLMs memorize sensitive training data, including personally identifiable information (PII), creating a pressing need for reliable post hoc removal methods. Unlearning has emerged as a promising solution, with state-of-the-art(SOTA) methods often following a localize-first, unlearn-second paradigm that targets specific model parameters. However, existing benchmarks evaluate unlearning solely at the output level, leaving open the question of whether unlearning truly erases knowledge from a model's parameters or merely obfuscates it, a concern reinforced by the success of resurfacing attacks. To bridge this gap, we introduce LACUNA: the first unlearning testbed with ground-truth parameter-level localization. LACUNA injects PII of synthetic individuals into predefined parameters of 1B and 7B OLMo-based models via masked continual pretraining, enabling direct evaluation of whether unlearning targets the weights responsible for knowledge storage. We use LACUNA to benchmark current SOTA unlearning methods and find that, despite strong output-level performance, existing methods are highly imprecise and susceptible to resurfacing attacks. We further show that when localization is successful, even a simple gradient-based unlearning method achieves strong erasure and robustness to resurfacing attacks, highlighting the importance of precise unlearning. We release LACUNA to complement behavioral evaluations and drive further advances in robust, localization-based unlearning.

</details>


### [47] [Mapping Text to Multiplex Graph: Prompt Compression as Lévy Walk-Guided Graph Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01241)
*Yaxin Gao,Yao Lu,Jinhong Deng,Jiaqi Nie,Zhe Tang,Jian Zhang,Zhaowei Zhu,Shanqing Yu,Qi Xuan,Joey Tianyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 RAGP（冗余感知图剪枝）方法，将提示词压缩建模为多层图上的冗余感知剪枝问题，利用 Lévy 游走在异构图结构中平衡局部挖掘与全局探索，实现高效的非冗余节点识别。实验表明，在 4 倍压缩比下 RAGP 平均得分 49.3，超越现有 LLM 压缩基线及视觉文本压缩范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示词压缩方法将文本视为扁平标记序列，无法捕捉分布在多个位置并通过局部句法依赖和全局语义关系连接的重要信息。这种关系结构天然可表示为图，其中标记或句子为节点，依赖为边。

Method: 提出 RAGP，将提示词压缩形式化为多层图上的冗余感知图剪枝。该图联合建模细粒度注意力依赖和粗粒度语义关系。为高效识别异构结构（稠密局部子图和稀疏全局连接）中的非冗余节点，采用 Lévy 游走，其重尾步长分布自然平衡局部开发与全局探索。

Result: 在 LongBench 基准上，RAGP 在 4 倍压缩比下达到平均分 49.3，优于 LongLLMLingua 在 3 倍压缩比下的 48.8。同时在多个任务上超越最先进的基于视觉的文本压缩范式。

Conclusion: RAGP 通过多层图建模和 Lévy 游走实现了有效的提示词压缩，在更高压缩比下保持更优性能，验证了图结构与随机游走策略在冗余感知压缩中的优势。

Abstract: Existing prompt compression methods treat text as flat token sequences, failing to capture the distributed nature of important information, which is often spread across multiple locations and connected through both local syntactic dependencies and global semantic relations. Such relational structure is naturally represented as a graph, where tokens or sentences become nodes and their dependencies become edges. To this end, we propose RAGP, which formulates prompt compression as Redundancy-Aware Graph Pruning on a multiplex graph that jointly models fine-grained attention-based dependencies and coarse-grained semantic relations. To efficiently identify non-redundant nodes in this heterogeneous structure (dense local subgraphs and sparse global connections), we employ Levy walks whose heavy-tailed step distribution naturally balances local exploitation with global exploration. Experiments on LongBench show that RAGP achieves an average score of 49.3 under a 4x compression ratio, outperforming existing LLM-based compression methods, such as LongLLMLingua, which attains 48.8 at a 3x compression ratio. Besides, RAGP also surpasses state-of-the-art vision-based text compression paradigms on multiple tasks. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/RAGP-B0CB.

</details>


### [48] [TurnNat: Automatic Evaluation of Turn-Taking Naturalness in Dyadic Spoken Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01345)
*Hao Zhang,Thomas Thebaud,Georgi Tinchev,Venkatesh Ravichandran,Laureano Moro-Velazquez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TurnNat框架，基于似然自动评估双通道口语对话中的轮流发言自然度，通过因果预测模型计算负对数似然，在受控扰动基准上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮流发言自然度对全双工口语对话系统至关重要，但自动评估受限；现有方法依赖人工判断或特定时序指标，难以在统一框架下比较异构时序故障。

Method: 提出TurnNat框架，训练因果轮流发言预测模型估计未来双人语音活动状态，用观测未来活动的负对数似然（NLL）度量时序异常性；将帧级NLL池化到从话语起止提取的轮流发言边界单元（TBUs），聚合均值和尾部TBU得分得到对话级自然度分数；构建受控扰动基准（自然与扰动对话片段对），经人工自然度判断验证。

Result: 在基准实验中，TurnNat成功识别跨异构时序故障的非自然轮流发言扰动。

Conclusion: TurnNat提供了一个统一的似然基础框架，能自动评估轮流发言自然度，且在受控扰动基准上表现优异，为全双工口语对话系统评估提供了新工具。

Abstract: Turn-taking naturalness is central to full-duplex spoken dialogue systems, yet its automatic evaluation remains limited. Existing evaluations often rely on human judgments or behavior-specific timing metrics, making it difficult to compare heterogeneous timing failures within a unified framework. We propose TurnNat, a likelihood-based framework for automatic turn-taking naturalness evaluation in two-channel spoken dialogue. A causal turn-taking prediction model trained on natural conversations estimates future two-speaker voice-activity states, and the negative log-likelihood (NLL) of the observed future activity measures timing atypicality. TurnNat pools frame-level NLLs over turn-taking boundary units (TBUs) extracted from utterance onsets and offsets, and aggregates mean and tail TBU scores into a dialogue-level naturalness score. We further construct a controlled perturbation benchmark of paired natural and perturbed dialogue clips, validated by human naturalness judgments. Experiments on this benchmark show that TurnNat successfully identifies unnatural turn-taking perturbations across heterogeneous timing failures.

</details>


### [49] [MultAttnAttrib: Training-Free Multimodal Attribution in Long Document Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01420)
*Dang Quang Thien Tran,Quang V. Dang,Vinamra Tyagi,Sai Soorya Rao Veeravalli,Trang Nguyen,Ryan A. Rossi,Franck Dernoncourt,Nedim Lipka,Koustava Goswami,Samyadeep Basu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的多模态归因生成方法 MultAttnAttrib，利用模型的 prefill pass、选定注意力头和校准阈值定位文档证据，并构建了首个面向长文档多模态归因的评测数据集 MultAttrEval。实验显示该方法优于多种基线（包括强提示方法），性能达到 GPT-5.4 级别，且推理延迟仅为提示方法的 1/7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于事实的问答系统在 AI 助手中广泛部署，准确将生成答案归因于证据对用户信任和模型安全至关重要。单模态归因已深入研究，但多模态场景研究较少，亟需一种针对多模态长文档的归因生成方法及评估基准。

Method: MultAttnAttrib 是一种免训练归因生成方法，利用模型的 prefill 阶段、选定的注意力头和校准阈值来定位文档中的源证据。同时推出 MultAttrEval 数据集，提供细粒度真实归因标注，专门用于多模态长文档归因评测。

Result: 实验表明 MultAttnAttrib 持续优于多种归因生成方法（包括强提示基线），性能匹配最新前沿模型如 GPT-5.4。该方法显著提升单模态和多模态归因准确性，且推理延迟仅为同一基础模型上提示方法的 1/7。

Conclusion: MultAttnAttrib 为多模态归因提供了高效、免训练的解决方案，配合首个专门针对长文档多模态归因的评测基准 MultAttrEval，推动了该领域研究。该方法在准确性和效率上均表现优异，适合实际部署。

Abstract: As grounded QA systems are increasingly deployed in AI assistants, accurately attributing generated answers to evidence is critical for user trust and model safety. While unimodal attributions have been explored in depth, the multimodal setting remains relatively under-researched. As a result, we introduce MultAttnAttrib, a training-free attribution-generation method that leverages a model's prefill pass, selected attention heads, and calibrated thresholds to locate source evidence within a document. To establish baseline results for the method, we introduce MultAttrEval, a complementary benchmark dataset annotated with fine-grained, ground-truth attributions for answer components grounded in multimodal source documents. To our knowledge, this is the first evaluation dataset designed specifically for multimodal attribution in long-form documents. Experimental results show that MultAttnAttrib consistently outperforms a variety of attribution-generation methods, including several strong prompting-based approaches and matches the latest frontier models such as GPT 5.4. Our method not only substantially improves attribution accuracy for both unimodal and multimodal attribution types, but also produces attributions at up to one-seventh of the direct inference latency compared to prompting on the same base model.

</details>


### [50] [IsoSci: A Benchmark of Isomorphic Cross-Domain Science Problems for Evaluating Reasoning versus Knowledge Retrieval in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01431)
*Samir Abdaljalil,Erchin Serpedin,Hasan Kurban*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ISOSCI基准测试揭示LLM推理模式增益主要依赖领域知识而非结构不变推理，挑战了思维链推理提升科学问题解决能力的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估基准混淆了推理能力与领域知识检索，难以单独评估推理能力的迁移性。

Method: 构建同构跨领域科学问题对基准ISOSCI，每对问题共享相同逻辑结构但需不同领域知识，跨五个模型对四大模型家族进行对比实验。

Result: 91.3%推理模式增益为知识依赖而非结构不变（63/69增益，Wilson 95% CI [82.3%, 96.0%]）；高能力模型推理切换增益<5pp；推理专用模型o3-mini在GPQA Diamond领先+19.2pp却在ISOSCI落后-24.7pp。

Conclusion: 推理增益高度依赖领域知识，基准选择决定推理效用结论，ISOSCI为分离推理与知识提供受控评估工具。

Abstract: We introduce ISOSCI, a benchmark of isomorphic cross-domain science problem pairs that separates reasoning ability from domain knowledge retrieval in LLM evaluation. Each pair shares identical logical structure but requires different domain-specific knowledge, enabling controlled attribution of reasoning-mode gains. Across five model pairs spanning four model families, we find that 91.3% of reasoning-mode gains are knowledge-dependent rather than structure-invariant (63/69 gains; Wilson 95% CI [82.3%, 96.0%]), directly challenging the assumption that chain-of-thought reasoning improves short-horizon procedural scientific problem-solving. Reasoning toggles on highly capable models provide less than 5 percentage points accuracy gain across all domains, and a reasoning-specialized model (o3-mini) that outperforms its standard counterpart on GPQA Diamond (+19.2 percentage points) underperforms on ISOSCI (-24.7 percentage points), showing that benchmark choice determines conclusions about reasoning utility. We release ISOSCI at https://huggingface.co/datasets/isosci/isosci

</details>


### [51] [Grounded Optimization: A Layered Engineering Framework for Reducing LLM Hallucination in Automated Personal Document Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01457)
*Shashank Indukuri,Adarsh Agrawal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 Grounded Optimization 五层框架，解决大语言模型在简历优化中产生的四类幻觉（时代错位技术注入、跨领域术语污染、结构突变、内容捏造）。在 3 个模型、4 个温度、6 种层配置、25 份合成简历（14 行业）的消融实验中，基线每份简历产生 2.48-5.36 个幻觉；时间幻觉减少 50-95%，整体检测幻觉率降至 0.04-0.24。提示词级 grounding 在低温且强指令跟随模型下可达零幻觉；高温或弱模型需确定性层补充。同时开源污染分类、评测代码和原始数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地用于面向申请人跟踪系统（ATS）的简历优化，但会产生与普通文本生成不同的幻觉：时代错位技术注入、跨领域术语污染、结构突变、内容捏造，严重影响简历真实性与 ATS 通过率。现有方法缺乏针对性的多层防御机制，亟需系统性框架来消除这些领域特定幻觉。

Method: 提出 Grounded Optimization 五层框架：(1) 时间上下文验证，确保技术栈与时间线一致；(2) 确定性污染检测，基于规则识别跨领域术语滥用；(3) 结构不变性强制，保持简历章节与格式完整；(4) 提示词级 grounding，将真实简历内容作为约束注入提示词；(5) 评估器代理，综合上层输出进行最终校验。通过消融实验在 3 个 LLMs、4 个温度、6 种层组合、25 份合成简历（覆盖 14 行业）上系统评估各层贡献。

Result: 无防御基线每份简历产生 2.48-5.36 个检测幻觉。时间幻觉在所有条件下减少 50-95%，整体检测幻觉率降至 0.04-0.24。仅提示词级 grounding 在低温且强指令跟随模型下即可实现零检测幻觉；高温或弱模型下需确定性层（时间验证、污染检测、结构强制）作为必要补充。实验表明五层协同最稳健。

Conclusion: Grounded Optimization 通过分层防御有效消除简历优化中的领域特定幻觉。提示词级 grounding 是核心，但确定性层对鲁棒性至关重要。开源的污染分类、评测代码和数据为后续研究提供基准。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to resume optimization for applicant tracking systems, introducing hallucination failures distinct from general text generation: anachronistic technology injection, cross-domain terminology contamination, structural mutation, and content fabrication. We present Grounded Optimization, a five-layer framework combining temporal context validation, deterministic contamination detection, structural invariant enforcement, prompt-level grounding, and an evaluator agent.
  In ablation experiments across three LLMs, four temperature settings, and six layer configurations on 25 synthetic resumes spanning 14 industries, undefended baselines produce 2.48-5.36 detected hallucinations per resume. Among detectors independent of the active defenses, temporal hallucinations are reduced by 50-95% across all conditions; overall detected hallucination rate falls to 0.04-0.24. Prompt-level grounding alone achieves zero detected hallucinations at low temperature with a capable instruction-following model; higher temperatures and weaker models reveal the need for the deterministic layers as a complement. We release the contamination taxonomy, evaluation code, and raw data.

</details>

### [52] [Multilayer Q-Matrix-Embedded Neural Network for Cognitive Diagnosis (M-QCDNet): Structure-Aware Deep Learning Architecture for Psychometric Interpretability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01278)
*Yiyao Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多层Q矩阵嵌入神经网络（M-QCDNet），将认知诊断模型的结构可解释性与深度学习神经网络的灵活性相结合，实现了可解释、公平且可操作的认知诊断AI。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知诊断模型虽具心理测量透明度但缺乏神经网络的灵活性，而纯深度学习模型虽预测性能强但缺乏结构可解释性。研究旨在弥合心理测量透明度与神经网络灵活性之间的鸿沟，构建兼具诊断效度与预测能力的认知诊断模型。

Method: 1. 利用Q矩阵作为结构先验，将项目-技能关系嵌入神经网络架构，确保潜在掌握轮廓的可解释性与认知理论一致；2. 设计带L2惩罚项的损失函数，惩罚与Q矩阵不一致的技能激活，平衡预测性能与结构对齐；3. 开发基于可解释对齐的评价指标，量化预测技能激活与项目层面技能的对应程度。

Result: M-QCDNet在保持认知诊断模型结构可解释性的同时，实现了深度学习的预测灵活性；提出的对齐指标有效量化了技能激活的一致性；模型能够支持课堂实践中的学习困难早期检测和基于掌握程度的干预。

Conclusion: 通过将诊断效度嵌入模型设计，M-QCDNet成功连接了心理测量透明度与神经网络灵活性，推动了可解释、公平且可操作的认知诊断AI发展，为教育测量与智能辅导系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: The research proposes a multilayer Q-matrix-embedded neural network for cognitive diagnosis (M-QCDNet), which integrates the structural interpretability of cognitive diagnostic models (CDMs) with the deep learning neural network (NN). M-QCDNet structures the item-skill relationship using the Q-matrix as a structural prior, ensuring latent mastery profiles remain interpretable and consistent with cognitive theory, followed by the proposed loss function with an L2 penalty to penalize skills not aligned with the Q-matrix and to balance predictive performance and structural alignment. Corresponding evaluation matrices, the interpretable alignment-based metrics that quantify the degree to which predicted skill activations correspond to item-level skills, were further developed. M-QCDNet offers practical benefits for classroom practice, enabling early detection of learning difficulties and supporting mastery-based interventions. By embedding diagnostic validity into model design, M-QCDNet bridges psychometric transparency and neural flexibility, advancing interpretable, fair, and actionable AI for cognitive diagnostics.

</details>


### [53] [I\textsuperscript{2}RiMA: Spectral Riemannian Representation with Temporal Attention for Mental Stress Detection based on EEG Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01279)
*Cheng He,Kunyu Peng,Shangen Han,Jinming Ma,Jinhong Ding,Likun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于跨被试EEG压力检测的Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network（IIRMAN），通过在频域构建空间协方差矩阵并映射到SPD切空间，结合频率簇聚合和切片内外注意力机制，有效保留频率特异性判别模式和时间相干性，在三个数据集上以1.60M参数和31.95M FLOPs的高效模型实现82.78%平衡准确率，超越五个SOTA基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨被试EEG压力检测面临判别性压力相关模式既依赖被试又依赖频率的挑战。传统Riemannian方法仅在时域建模空间协方差，忽略了对高级认知状态解码至关重要的神经振荡；标准时间切片化往往破坏切片间的时间相干性。现有方法未能同时保留频率特异性判别线索和跨序列的全局时间上下文。

Method: 提出Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network（IIRMAN）：1) 在每个频率点独立构建空间协方差矩阵并映射到SPD切空间，保留通道级几何结构与频率特异性判别线索；2) 引入频率簇聚合，形成与EEG节律对齐的紧凑数据驱动频率簇，选择信息量大的频谱成分并减少冗余；3) 设计切片内外注意力模块，自适应融合局部切片级频谱动态与全局时间上下文。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上实验，IIRMAN持续优于五个最先进基线，最高达到82.78%平衡准确率，同时模型仅含1.60M参数和31.95M FLOPs，展现出优异的计算效率。

Conclusion: IIRMAN通过将Riemannian几何扩展到频域并结合层次化注意力机制，有效解决了跨被试EEG压力检测中频率特异性与时间相干性建模的难题，在保持轻量化的前提下达到SOTA性能，为基于EEG的认知状态解码提供了新范式。

Abstract: Cross-subject EEG stress detection remains challenging because discriminative stress-related patterns are both subject-dependent and frequency-specific. Conventional Riemannian methods model spatial covariance mainly in the time domain, overlooking neural oscillations that are critical for high-level cognitive state decoding, while standard temporal tokenization often fragments inter-slice temporal coherence. To address these limitations, we propose \method{}, an Intra-Inter Riemannian Manifold Attention Network for EEG-based stress detection. \method{} constructs spatial covariance matrices independently at each frequency point and maps them to the SPD tangent space, preserving channel-wise geometry together with frequency-specific discriminative cues. It further introduces frequency cluster aggregation to select informative spectral components and reduce redundancy by forming compact, data-driven frequency clusters aligned with EEG rhythms. Finally, an intra-inter slice attention module adaptively integrates local slice-level spectral dynamics and global temporal context across EEG sequences. Experiments on three datasets show that \method{} consistently outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines, achieving up to 82.78\% balanced accuracy while remaining efficient with only 1.60M parameters and 31.95M FLOPs.

</details>


### [54] [Fixed-Set Robustness in Programming by Example: Example Corruption and Semantic Partition Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01280)
*Yuan Si,Jialu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了编程示例系统 (PBE) 在对抗性示例损坏下的鲁棒性，提出了版本空间分区聚合 (VPA) 防御机制，并通过多种基准测试表明：低置信度 PBE 任务存在被随机噪声评估遗漏的对抗性脆弱性，而语义分区聚合仅在干净语义保持分区投票优势时有效，这在现实任务中常失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的鲁棒 PBE 工作主要将错误示例建模为随机噪声过程，最小化期望或经验损失。本文关注不同的失效模式：对手观察综合器并选择损坏示例以最大程度破坏返回程序。这揭示了随机 typo 和噪声 PBE 评估所忽视的对抗性鲁棒性维度。

Method: 1. 形式化固定集合最坏情况损坏，针对有限 PBE 版本空间。 2. 实现有界池内的精确损坏搜索和启发式损坏搜索，针对字符串转换 DSL。 3. 提出版本空间分区聚合 (VPA) 防御：在不相交示例组上综合并按语义签名投票。 4. 在精选/生成 DSL 任务、公开 SyGuS PBE_SLIA 切片、SYNTRA Playgol v2 和噪声 PBE 目标基线上进行评估。

Result: 1. 单个精选编辑即可翻转所有 8 个 spike 任务，而 200 次 typo、DSL 池和距离匹配的随机控制仅成功 10.3%、11.0% 和 16.7%。 2. 生成的 margin-1 行在预算 1 下翻转，VPA 可恢复。 3. 公开 SyGuS 上投票优势接近 1，自适应攻击者将 VPA 准确率降至零。 4. 公开 SyGuS 切片跨越精确有界池预算边界。 5. Playgol 在 141 个接受行上对 typo 和同池随机控制显示正向成对 bootstrap 差距。 6. 20 个受控 margin-1 任务上的小规模精确输出提示实验显示相同定性模式，跨本地和 API 模型一致。

Conclusion: 低置信度 PBE 任务存在被随机噪声评估遗漏的对抗性鲁棒性维度。语义分区聚合 (VPA) 仅在干净语义保持分区投票优势时有效，这在现实任务中常失效。对抗性鲁棒性需要显式建模最坏情况损坏，而非仅依赖随机噪声假设。

Abstract: Programming-by-example systems infer programs from a small set of input-output examples. Robust PBE work usually models wrong examples as samples from a stochastic noise process and then minimizes an expected or empirical loss. This paper studies a different failure mode: an adversary who sees the synthesizer and chooses the examples whose corruption most damages the returned program. We formalize fixed-set worst-case corruption for finite PBE version spaces, implement exact-within-bounded-pool and heuristic corruption searches for a string-transformation DSL, and introduce version-space partition aggregation (VPA), a defense that synthesizes on disjoint example groups and votes by semantic signatures. The central claim is deliberately bounded and partly negative: low-margin PBE tasks have an adversarial robustness dimension that random-typo and noisy-PBE evaluations miss, while semantic partition aggregation helps only when the clean semantics keep a partition vote margin, which often fails on realistic tasks. Evidence from curated/generated DSL tasks, accepted public SyGuS PBE_SLIA slices, SYNTRA Playgol v2, and noisy-PBE objective baselines supports that boundary. One curated edit flips all 8 spike tasks while 200-trial typo, DSL-pool, and distance-matched random controls succeed on 10.3%, 11.0%, and 16.7%; generated margin-1 rows flip under budget 1 yet VPA recovers them; on public SyGuS the vote margin is near one, so an adaptive attacker drives VPA accuracy to zero; accepted public SyGuS slices move across exact-within-pool budget boundaries; and Playgol shows positive paired-bootstrap gaps against typo and same-pool random controls on the 141 accepted rows. A small exact-output prompt harness over 20 controlled margin-1 tasks shows the same qualitative clean-to-attacked pattern across local and API models, while it is treated as a scope check, not a broad LLM benchmark.

</details>


### [55] [Domain Knowledge Based Temporal-Spatial Graph Convolution Network for ECG Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01282)
*Wenting Ma,Zhipeng Zhang,Xiaohang Yuan,Ningwei Xie,Yuxin Xie,Xiaolin Wang,Meng Guo,Xingang Chai,Zhenjie Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于领域知识的图卷积网络用于心电图（ECG）识别，结合PRQST关键点作为领域知识，利用双流有向图建模心电周期内部和周期间的空间位置关系与时间依赖，在首届中国心电智能大赛数据集上取得了优于现有模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI模型在医疗等专业领域的可解释性不足，特别是心电图识别中，单纯依赖端到端卷积神经网络难以充分利用领域知识，导致罕见类别检测性能较差。

Method: 引入PRQST关键点作为领域知识，构建双流有向图：空间有向图捕捉关键点间的位置关系，时间有向图建模相邻心电周期间的时间依赖，结合图卷积网络进行心电图分类。

Result: 在首届中国心电智能大赛数据集（九分类任务）上，整体平均F1分数达到88.1%，罕见类别平均F1分数为76.3%，均优于最先进模型，领域知识的引入显著提升了罕见类别的检测性能。

Conclusion: 基于领域知识的图卷积网络能有效融合心电图专业知识，通过双流有向图建模时空依赖，显著提升心电图分类性能，尤其是对罕见类别的识别，为可解释的医疗AI提供了新思路。

Abstract: In light of strides in Arti cial Intelligence (AI) and its wide spread application, challenges persist in the interpretability of AI models, particularly within specialized domains like healthcare, such as electro cardiograph (ECG) recognition. Rather than relying solely on end-to-end convolutional neural networks, this paper introduces a novel approach using a domain knowledge-based graph convolution network for ECG recognition. Key landmarks points of PRQST, vital to ECG interpreta tion, are incorporated as domain knowledge. The double-stream directed graph is employed to model both intra and inter ECG cycles. Speci cally, spatial directed graphs capture the positional relationships among key points, while temporal directed graphs delineate temporal dependencies between adjacent cycles in extended ECG sequences. Experimental re sults on the First Chinese ECG Intelligent Competition dataset, which speci cally classify ECG into nine categories, prove the e cacy of the proposed model. The overall average F1 score is 88.1%, the average F1 score of rare categories is 76.3%, both outperform the state-of-the-art models. The introduction of domain knowledge did enhance the detec tion performance, especially for rare categories.

</details>


### [56] [Scaling Laws for Grid-Based Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search in High Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01283)
*Matthew J Liu,Wei Hang Zheng,Vidhan Purohit,Siqi Xie,Chieh-En Li,Jerry Li,Noah Flynn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文系统分析了多探针网格算法在近似最近邻搜索中的表现，发现其在高维数据上保持恒定的维度缩放指数，且查询随N近线性缩放，索引成本较低，适合重建频繁或高维场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代缩放分析中缺乏基于网格的ANN方法研究，需要系统表征多探针网格算法在数据集大小N和维度d上的性能。

Method: 实验系统表征多探针网格算法，在GloVe嵌入族上对比图、树、分区等方法，分析N和d的缩放性质。

Result: 发现d缩放交叉现象：多探针网格搜索维持近似恒定的维度缩放指数，其他方法吞吐量下降；查询随N近线性缩放，索引成本更低。

Conclusion: 网格方法如多探针网格在重建密集或高维设置中具有竞争力；ANN算法的N和d缩放性质可指导高效Transformer架构的成本分析。

Abstract: Grid-based approaches to approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search have been absent from modern scaling analyses. We present a systematic characterization of a multiprobe grid algorithm with respect to dataset size $N$ and dimensionality $d$. Our experiments reveal a previously unreported $d$-scaling crossover on the GloVe embedding family, in which multiprobe grid search maintains an approximately constant dimensional scaling exponent while other graph-, tree-, and partitioning-based methods exhibit degrading throughput. The advantage comes with near-linear query scaling in $N$, but also with lower indexing cost than competing ANN methods. Our results suggest that grid-based methods such as multiprobe grid may be competitive in rebuild-heavy or high-dimensional settings where indexing cost and dimensional robustness dictate performance. More broadly, recent work has formalized self-attention as an ANN operation. Thus, the $N$- and $d$-scaling properties of ANN algorithms may guide cost analysis of efficient transformer architectures. Code is available at: https://github.com/weiz345/MultiProbeANN.

</details>

Conclusion: IonSense-QKG 将数据集选择定位为数据管理问题，通过标准化的量子就绪元数据和评分机制，为混合量子-经典电池分析提供了可复现的基础设施，推动了数据中心的量子电池研究。

Abstract: Public lithium-ion battery datasets are increasingly used for state-of-health estimation, remaining-useful-life prediction, anomaly detection, electrochemical diagnostics, second-life analytics, and battery safety research. However, these datasets vary substantially in chemistry, modality, scale, label quality, sequence structure, access status, and preprocessing complexity. These differences directly affect whether a dataset is feasible for near-term hybrid quantum-classical machine-learning workflows.
  This paper presents IonSense-QKG, a quantum-readiness metadata framework for lithium-ion battery dataset discovery. Starting from the EV-Battery-IonSense index, the proposed framework enriches public battery dataset records with quantum-relevant metadata, including task type, sensing modality, chemistry, label availability, sequence type, preprocessing requirements, candidate quantum encodings, estimated qubit range, and NISQ feasibility. A transparent Quantum Readiness Score is introduced to rank datasets as candidate resources for future hybrid quantum-classical battery benchmarks. The score is intended as a dataset-selection heuristic, not as evidence of quantum advantage.
  The framework demonstrates query-based discovery over enriched metadata to identify datasets suitable for compact quantum feature maps, quantum time-series workflows, limited-label anomaly detection, and future battery-health benchmarking. The released artifact includes metadata tables, scoring scripts, robustness checks, link-checking utilities, and SQL-style query examples. IonSense-QKG positions dataset selection as a data-management problem and provides a reproducible foundation for data-centric quantum battery analytics.

</details>

*Paulo R. Ferreira,Lucas Coutinho Freitas,Laís dos Santos Gonçalves,William Borges Domingues,Lucas Petitemberte de Souza,Mariana B. Michalowski,Vinicius F. Campos*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DNA甲基化谱分析在CNS肿瘤分类中存在跨队列迁移性、方法学正确性和稳健多类别评估等挑战，需要更严谨的机器学习方法来提高分类可靠性。

Method: 结合稀疏随机投影进行降维，使用多项式逻辑回归进行分类。在参考队列（2,801样本）上采用分层3折交叉验证，在独立临床评估队列（1,104样本）上验证。

Result: 参考队列上平均准确率96%；独立队列上91类别级准确率86%，甲基化类别家族级准确率93%，分别比现有最先进方法的82%和88%提升约4和5个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法基于更强的机器学习方法学实践，在多种评估设置下持续优于现有最先进方法，能显著提高CNS肿瘤分类的可靠性，且临床相关性强，可影响治疗选择和临床决策。

Abstract: NA methylation profiling has become a powerful approach for central nervous system (CNS) tumor classification, yet important challenges remain regarding cross-cohort transferability, methodological correctness, and robust multiclass evaluation. In this work, we propose a novel and methodologically rigorous machine-learning approach for methylation-based CNS tumor classification that combines Sparse Random Projection for dimensionality reduction with multinomial logistic regression for classification. We evaluate the proposed approach in the same general experimental setting established by a widely used reference classifier. On the 2,801-sample reference cohort, our method achieves a mean accuracy of 96\% under stratified 3-fold cross-validation. On the independent 1,104-sample clinical evaluation cohort, it reaches 86\% accuracy at the 91-class level and 93\% when predictions are evaluated at the methylation class family level. These results improve upon the corresponding state-of-the-art reference figures of 82\% class-level concordance and 88\% family-level concordance, yielding absolute gains of approximately 4 and 5 percentage points, respectively. This improvement is clinically relevant: in a diagnostic setting, a 5-point increase in correct tumor classification can directly affect cancer subtype assignment and, in turn, influence treatment selection and downstream clinical decision-making. Our results show that the proposed model, grounded in stronger methodological practice in machine learning, consistently outperforms the previous state of the art across evaluation settings and can materially improve the reliability of CNS tumor classification.

</details>


### [59] [From Approximation to Emergence: A Theory of Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01311)
*Zhilin Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这是一本关于深度学习理论的专著，提供了从经典近似、优化、泛化理论到现代过参数化、鲁棒性、生成建模、Transformer、上下文学习、缩放定律、可解释性、对齐和涌现现象的统一、面向证明的理论框架。本书将分散的研究整理为连贯的研究叙事，适合研究者、研究生和数学背景从业者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习已超越任何单一数学解释的范畴，现有理论分散且缺乏统一框架。需要一部系统性著作，将从经典基础到前沿机制的广泛文献组织成连贯的研究叙事，厘清各理论控制的对象、有效假设及未解释现象，为深度学习理论提供严谨的全景图谱。

Method: 采用专著形式，按理论控制对象、有效假设、未解释现象三个维度系统梳理文献。从近似论、优化理论、泛化理论等经典基础出发，逐步展开至过参数化、鲁棒性、生成建模、Transformer架构、上下文学习、缩放定律、可解释性、对齐及涌现等当代核心机制，构建面向证明的统一理论叙事。

Result: 完成了深度学习理论的系统性整合与叙事重构，形成了一部涵盖从基础到前沿的严谴图谱。明确了当前理论体系的强大性、不完备性及核心问题：学习机制如何从规模、数据、架构与训练中涌现。为后续研究提供了结构化的理论基座与问题导向。

Conclusion: 本书确立了深度学习理论的统一研究范式，揭示了理论发展的内在逻辑主线：从静态近似向动态涌现的演进。指出当前核心挑战在于解释规模、数据、架构、训练四要素交互产生的涌现机制，为未来理论突破指明了方向。

Abstract: Deep learning has outgrown any single mathematical explanation. From Approximation to Emergence develops a unified, proof-oriented account of modern deep learning theory, tracing a path from the classical foundations of approximation, optimization, and generalization to the contemporary mechanisms of overparameterization, robustness, generative modeling, transformers, in-context learning, scaling laws, interpretability, alignment, and emergence. Rather than presenting isolated results, the book organizes a broad literature into a coherent research narrative: each theory is examined through the object it controls, the assumptions that make it valid, and the phenomena it leaves unexplained. Written for researchers, graduate students, and mathematically trained practitioners, this monograph offers a rigorous map of deep learning theory as it stands today: powerful, incomplete, and increasingly centered on the question of how learned mechanisms arise from scale, data, architecture, and training.

</details>


### [60] [NeuroBridge: Bridging Multi-Task MRI Knowledge for Neurodegenerative Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01401)
*Mengyu Li,Guoyao Shen,Chad W. Farris,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroBridge是一个临床指导的多任务MRI框架，通过大规模自监督预训练结合海马分割、萎缩分类和重建目标，实现了阿尔茨海默病、轻度认知障碍及相关痴呆的高精度诊断，并在跨队列泛化和机会性筛查中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确利用MRI识别阿尔茨海默病(AD)、轻度认知障碍(MCI)及相关痴呆仍具挑战，因为疾病相关的结构变化往往细微且异质；现有单任务方法受限，需要临床指导的多任务表示学习来提升诊断性能。

Method: NeuroBridge框架整合大规模自监督MRI预训练，结合海马分割、海马萎缩分类和重建目标，采用门控融合微调策略；在ADNI和OASIS队列上评估，包括跨队列迁移、基于概率的分析和机会性筛查。

Result: NeuroBridge在评估的分类任务中达到最高性能，AD与认知正常对照在ADNI上准确率88.17%，OASIS上82.78%；在MCI相关及混合诊断设置中增益最大；展现出强跨队列泛化、预测类别概率与准确率的系统关联，以及基于概率的机会性筛查可行性。

Conclusion: 临床指导的多任务表示学习超越传统单任务方法，改进了神经退行性疾病MRI诊断；NeuroBridge为痴呆评估和基于MRI的机会性筛查提供了鲁棒且可扩展的框架。

Abstract: INTRODUCTION: Accurate MRI-based identification of Alzheimer's disease (AD), mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and related dementias remains challenging because disease-related structural changes are often subtle and heterogeneous. We developed NeuroBridge, a clinically guided multi-task MRI framework for neurodegenerative disease diagnosis. METHODS: NeuroBridge integrates large-scale self-supervised MRI pretraining with hippocampal segmentation, hippocampal atrophy classification, and reconstruction objectives, followed by gated fusion fine-tuning. Performance was evaluated across ADNI and OASIS cohorts, including cross-cohort transfer, probability-based analysis, and opportunistic screening. RESULTS: NeuroBridge achieved the highest performance across evaluated classification tasks, reaching 88.17% accuracy for AD versus cognitively normal controls in ADNI and 82.78% in OASIS. The largest gains occurred in MCI-related and mixed-diagnosis settings. The framework demonstrated strong cross-cohort generalization, systematic associations between predicted-class probability and accuracy, and the feasibility of probability-based opportunistic screening. DISCUSSION: Clinically guided multi-task representation learning improves neurodegenerative MRI diagnosis beyond conventional single-task approaches. NeuroBridge provides a robust and scalable framework for dementia assessment and MRI-based opportunistic screening.

</details>


### [61] [Token Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01455)
*Kathan Shah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Ember，一种轻量级优化器，专门针对嵌入表和 LM-head 矩阵，仅需 O(V + D) 显存（Adam 需 O(2VD)），利用嵌入层独特的梯度几何特性，在监督微调、强化学习和预训练中改善帕累托前沿，且仅需千字节级优化器状态。实验表明 Ember 能随批次大小和参数量有效扩展，且 Token 的优化轨迹可用简单 1D 射线描述，挑战了神经网络参数在高度非凸景观中导航的传统认知。代码已开源并兼容 ZeRO/FSDP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有优化器（如 Adam）对嵌入表和 LM-head 矩阵维护完整的二阶动量，导致显存占用随词表大小 V 和隐藏维度 D 线性增长（O(2VD)），成为大规模训练的瓶颈。作者观察到嵌入层与密集隐藏层的梯度几何存在本质差异，且 Token 优化轨迹呈现低维结构，因此设计极低显存占用的专用优化器。

Method: Ember 仅为嵌入表和 LM-head 维护 O(V + D) 规模的优化器状态，利用梯度几何特性设计更新规则，避免分片 Token 表优化器状态。实现上与 ZeRO/FSDP 无缝集成，支持分布式训练。

Result: 在监督微调、RL 和预训练任务上，Ember 在仅用千字节优化器状态的情况下，达到或超越 Adam 的性能，且随批次大小和参数量良好扩展。实证发现 Token 优化轨迹可用 1D 射线揭示，揭示了 Transformer 训练中优化器设计空间的惊人狭窄性。

Conclusion: 嵌入层与 LM-head 的独特梯度几何允许极简优化器实现高效训练，Ember 为大规模语言模型训练提供了显存高效的解决方案，并为理解神经网络优化动力学提供了新视角。

Abstract: Language models learn continuous programs over discrete symbols, with the embedding table and LM-head acting as the read/write interface between them. We show that this interface has gradient geometry distinct from dense hidden weights which can be exploited to improve the Pareto frontier across supervised finetuning, RL, and pretraining, while only utilizing kilobytes of optimizer state. We introduce Ember, a lightweight optimizer for embedding and LM-head matrices that utilizes O(V + D) VRAM, instead of Adam's O(2VD), and forgoes the need to shard both token table optimizer states. We provide empirical evidence that Ember scales effectively across batch size and parameter count. We show that the optimization trajectory of tokens can be well described by a simple 1D ray, counter to the popular belief that neural net parameters navigate a heavily nonconvex landscape. We provide a principled view on the surprisingly narrow space of optimizers that suffice for Transformer training. Finally, we open-source our distributed Ember implementation that merges cleanly with existing ZeRO/FSDP setups to support further research at https://github.com/katop1234/ember

</details>


### [62] [Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum and Faster Hyperparameter Exploration to Tackle Class Imbalance in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01474)
*Haemin Park,Diego Klabjan,Martin W. Braun,Xiuqi Li,Balakrishnan Ananthanarayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 FedCGNM 优化器解决联邦学习中的类别不平衡问题，通过类别分组、分组动量归一化求和来均衡梯度幅度并抑制少数类梯度噪声；同时引入基于 X-armed-bandit 的 FedHOO 算法优化时变重采样率，利用联邦并行性在小客户端规模下高效搜索。实验在四个公开长尾基准及专有芯片缺陷数据集上验证 FedCGNM 持续优于基线，FedHOO 在小规模联邦中进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中类别不平衡导致少数类预测性能差，且因隐私保护与数据异构约束，无法直接应用集中式重采样或加权损失等标准技术。

Method: 1. FedCGNM：按组内方差最小将类别划分为少量组，为每组维护动量并归一化为单位长度，用归一化分组动量之和作为更新方向，从而均衡多数/少数类梯度幅度并缓解稀有类梯度噪声。2. 理论收敛分析显式考虑时变重采样率。3. FedHOO：基于 X-armed-bandit (XAB) 的算法，利用联邦并行性以线性代价评估每客户端两个候选重采样率的组合，高效优化小客户端规模下的重采样率。

Result: 在四个公开长尾基准数据集和一个专有芯片缺陷数据集上，FedCGNM 稳定超越各类基线方法；结合 FedHOO 优化重采样率后，在小规模联邦学习场景中获得额外性能增益。

Conclusion: FedCGNM 通过分组动量归一化有效缓解联邦学习中的类别不平衡，FedHOO 进一步解决小客户端规模下重采样率优化难题，二者结合在理论与实验上均展现出优越性。

Abstract: Class imbalance poses a critical challenge in federated learning (FL), where underrepresented classes suffer from poor predictive performance yet cannot be addressed by standard centralized techniques due to privacy and heterogeneity constraints. We propose FedCGNM (Federated Class-Grouped Normalized Momentum), a client-side optimizer in FL that partitions classes into a small number of groups based on minimum within-group variance, maintains a momentum per group, normalizes each group momentum to unit length, and uses the summation of the normalized group momentums as an update direction. This design both equalizes gradient magnitude across majority and minority groups and mitigates the noise inherent in rare-class gradients. We further provide a theoretical convergence analysis explicitly accounting for time-varying resampling-rates. Additionally, to efficiently optimize these rates in small-client regimes, we introduce FedHOO, an X-armed-bandit (XAB) based algorithm that exploits federated parallelism that evaluates many combinations of two candidate rates per client at linear cost. Empirical evaluation on four public long-tailed benchmarks and a proprietary chip-defect dataset demonstrates that FedCGNM consistently outperforms baselines, with FedHOO yielding further gains in small-scale federations.

</details>


### [63] [How to Allocate Your Tokens? Scaling Laws with Training Steps and Batch Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01487)
*Fabian Schaipp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出三项式缩放定律，将训练数据显式拆分为训练步数和批量大小，能准确恢复最优批量大小的缩放行为，且利用次优批量大小的训练运行即可用显著更少的实验鲁棒拟合，还可推导次优批量大小的缩放定律，与关键批量大小的实证发现一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放定律通常将训练数据视为整体，未显式区分训练步数和批量大小，导致需要大量训练运行才能拟合，且难以捕捉最优批量大小随模型规模变化的缩放规律。

Method: 提出三项式缩放定律，显式将训练数据分解为训练步数和批量大小两个项；在大规模训练运行集合上拟合该定律，并验证其对最优批量大小缩放的恢复能力、拟合数据效率及对次优批量大小缩放的预测性能。

Result: 三项式定律正确恢复了最优批量大小的缩放关系；利用次优批量大小的训练运行，该定律可用显著更少的实验运行实现鲁棒拟合；进一步推导出的次优批量大小缩放定律与已有关于关键批量大小的实证发现相符。

Conclusion: 三项式缩放定律为联合建模模型规模、训练步数和批量大小提供了更精确且数据高效的框架，有助于指导大规模训练中的超参数选择和资源分配。

Abstract: We propose a scaling law that takes into account model size and training data while explicitly splitting the latter into training steps and batch size (called three-term law). Fitting the proposed law on a large set of training runs, we find that it correctly recovers the scaling of the optimal batch size. Moreover, because it makes use of training runs with suboptimal batch size, our proposed law can be robustly fit with a significantly smaller amount of training runs. We further show that the three-term law can be used to derive scaling laws for suboptimal batch sizes, and that it matches previous empirical findings related to the critical batch size.

</details>


### [64] [Don't Let Gains FADE: Breaking Down Policy Gradient Weights in RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01490)
*Juliette Decugis,Sean O'Brien,Francis Bach,Gabriel Synnaeve,Taco Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出统一框架分析优势函数，设计自适应优势FADE，显著提升LLM推理训练稳定性与多样性，加速收敛并达成最佳准确性-多样性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习后训练虽能提升LLM推理，但面临训练不稳定和多样性崩塌问题；现有优势函数方法繁多，缺乏统一理解和选择指导。

Method: 提出统一框架将优势函数分解为正负梯度质量沿正负号轴和难度轴；基于此设计FADE（Focal Advantage with Dynamic Entropy），根据训练动态自动调度梯度权重。

Result: FADE在7B模型上比最佳静态基线提前20k步达到峰值pass@1，在32B模型上提前2k步；在LiveCodeBench和AIME上所有pass@k指标均实现最佳准确性-多样性权衡。

Conclusion: 通过统一优势函数分析框架和自适应FADE方法，有效解决了RL后训练的不稳定性与多样性崩塌，实现了更快收敛和更优的性能-多样性平衡。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training dramatically improves LLM reasoning, but suffers from training instability and diversity collapse. Advantage functions offer an appealing fix: they reshape the training objective, reweight which rollouts drive learning, and are trivial to implement. Yet a proliferation of methods makes it unclear which advantage to use and when. We cut through the confusion with a unifying framework that decomposes any advantage into its positive and negative gradient mass along two orthogonal axes. On the sign axis, imbalanced updates collapse either entropy or weight geometry. On the difficulty axis, hard-problem focus sharpens signal but costs sample size. Both trade-offs shift during training: exploration favors balance and hard focus; exploitation favors suppression and medium focus. This motivates FADE (Focal Advantage with Dynamic Entropy), a self-adapting advantage that reads training dynamics to schedule the gradient weight automatically. FADE reaches peak pass@1 20k steps earlier than the best static baseline at the 7B scale and 2k steps earlier at the 32B , while achieving the best accuracy-diversity trade-off across all pass@k on LiveCodeBench and AIME.

</details>


### [65] [Unveiling the Non-Monotonic Effect of Privacy on Generalization under Byzantine Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01492)
*Thomas Boudou,Batiste Le Bars,Nirupam Gupta,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究分布式学习中拜占庭鲁棒性、局部差分隐私（LDP）与泛化误差之间的关系，发现三元悖论在泛化误差上不普遍成立，而是取决于隐私制度：强隐私（高噪声）下增加隐私反而降低泛化误差，无张力；弱隐私（低噪声）下张力重现，增加隐私损害泛化。通过算法稳定性的匹配上下界解释非单调行为，并用实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作确立了拜占庭鲁棒性、LDP和优化误差之间的三元悖论，但泛化误差的表现尚不明确。本文旨在探究该三元悖论是否延伸至泛化误差，并揭示隐私制度对鲁棒性与隐私张力的影响机制。

Method: 理论分析方面，建立拜占庭鲁棒分布式学习在LDP约束下的算法稳定性匹配上下界，证明泛化误差随隐私预算非单调变化的机制；实验方面，通过实证评估验证理论预测并进一步分析。

Result: 在高噪声（强隐私）制度下，增加隐私降低泛化误差，鲁棒性与隐私无张力；在低噪声（弱隐私）制度下，张力重现，增加隐私损害泛化。理论给出匹配的算法稳定性上下界，解释非单调行为，实验结果与理论一致。

Conclusion: 三元悖论不普遍适用于泛化误差，隐私制度起关键作用：强隐私下隐私反而助益泛化，弱隐私下则存在权衡。这为设计兼顾鲁棒性与隐私的分布式学习算法提供新见解。

Abstract: Recent work has established a fundamental trilemma between Byzantine robustness, local differential privacy (LDP), and optimization error in distributed learning. We show that this trilemma does not universally extend to generalization error, but instead depends critically on the privacy regime. Specifically, in the high-noise regime (strong privacy), we prove that increasing privacy reduces the generalization error, i.e., there is no tension between robustness and privacy. In the low-noise regime (weaker privacy), however, the tension between robustness and privacy reappears and increasing privacy indeed degrades generalization. Our theory explains this surprising non-monotonic behavior of the generalization error via matching lower and upper bounds on the algorithmic stability of Byzantine-robust distributed learning under LDP constraints. We corroborate and further analyze these theoretical findings with empirical evaluations.

</details>

Conclusion: 该工作为不完全信息博弈中的策略表示学习提供了系统性基准，代码已开源供后续扩展研究。

Abstract: We investigate the problem of learning useful policy representations (embeddings) in two-player zero-sum imperfect-information games. We make three contributions: First, we introduce methods of creating datasets of policies for a given game. Second, we propose methods to learn policy representations. Third, we introduce downstream tasks to evaluate the effectiveness of such representations.
  We evaluate each dataset method, embedding method, and downstream task on Kuhn and Leduc Poker. Although our methods are very basic, we demonstrate that useful behavioral representations are present in the learned embeddings. To our knowledge, this work is among the first to systematically compare self-supervised learning techniques for learning policy representations in games. Our code is available at https://github.com/VitamintK/ssl-project for others to extend.

</details>


### [67] [The risk of KV cache compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01520)
*Lukas Haverbeck,Carmen Amo Alonso,Andres Felipe Posada-Moreno,Sebastian Trimpe,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从理论层面分析了Transformer长序列推理中KV缓存压缩的极小极大风险，提出了基于内在可压缩性的设计原则，并构建了具有理论保证的实用压缩算法，在LongBench上取得良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存压缩方法主要依赖经验设计，理论研究仅证明最坏情况下压缩不可行，但缺乏对可压缩区域的系统指导。论文旨在弥合理论与实践的鸿沟，明确何时以及如何实现准确压缩。

Method: 通过表征KV缓存压缩的极小极大风险与内在可压缩性的关系，推导出因果掩码下的最优设计原则，将其高效映射到预填充和自回归解码阶段，实例化为一个实用压缩算法。

Result: 提出的算法在LongBench基准上的针对性实验中表现优异，验证了理论指导的压缩策略在实际长序列任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为KV缓存压缩提供了具有理论最优性保证的原则性途径，实现了从理论分析到实用算法的完整闭环。

Abstract: Transformer inference on long sequences is expensive because softmax attention repeatedly reads from a large KV cache. The prevalent approach to this bottleneck is KV cache compression, which replaces the full cache with a compact summary. Despite its practical importance, the design of such summaries is largely driven by empirical experimentation. On the theoretical side, existing results show that KV cache compression can be impossible in the worst case, but offer little systematic guidance for designing algorithms in regimes where accurate compression is possible. We bridge this gap by characterizing the minimax risk of KV cache compression in terms of the intrinsic compressibility of a cache, revealing when and how accurate compression is possible. These results yield novel design principles for KV cache compression under causal masking that map efficiently to prefill and autoregressive decoding while achieving minimax-optimal risk. We instantiate these principles in a practical algorithm and report promising performance on LongBench in targeted experiments. Overall, our results provide a principled avenue for practical KV cache compression with theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [68] [Multi-Head Recurrent Memory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01523)
*Jiatong Li,Samuel Yeh,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对循环记忆代理在长上下文下性能随长度增长而系统性下降的问题，将其分解为记忆捕获与记忆保持两因素，发现保持是主导瓶颈。提出多头循环记忆（MHM）框架，将记忆划分为独立头并采用分阶段选择-更新策略，使每步仅更新一个头而其余头结构性防覆写，并给出轻量化实例 MHM-LRU。实验表明，在 100K-1M token 范围内，MHM-LRU 显著提升保持率与端到端准确率，如 RULER-HQA 896K token 下保持率从 <30% 提升至 73.96%，且泛化性强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有循环记忆代理虽能扩展至任意长上下文，但端到端性能随上下文长度增长系统性劣化。作者通过分解性能为记忆捕获与记忆保持，量化确认保持是主导瓶颈：因记忆以整块文本维护，每次更新都有覆写先前内容的风险。

Method: 提出多头循环记忆（MHM）这一免训练通用框架：将固定大小记忆窗口划分为多个独立头，采用分阶段“选择-再更新”策略，每步仅选一个头进行更新，其余头在结构上被屏蔽避免被覆写，将保持负担从模型行为转移到架构设计。并给出轻量实例化 MHM-LRU（Least-Recently-Updated MHM），按最久未更新原则选择头，保证头利用均匀且零额外 token 开销。

Result: 在长上下文基准上，MHM-LRU 在 100K-1M token 区间大幅提升记忆保持率与端到端准确率，基线方法急剧下降。RULER-HQA 896K token 任务中，保持率从 <30% 提升至 73.96%。增益跨模型家族、规模与任务类型泛化，验证架构优化是实现可靠长上下文循环记忆的实用且低成本路径。

Conclusion: 通过架构层面的多头划分与选择性更新机制，MHM 框架有效解决了循环记忆的保持瓶颈，MHM-LRU 以零额外开销显著提升长上下文性能，证明架构优化是通往可靠长上下文循环记忆的务实高效方案。

Abstract: Recurrent memory agents extend LLMs to arbitrarily long contexts by iteratively consolidating input into a fixed-size memory window. Despite their scalability, these agents exhibit a well-documented reliability problem: end-to-end performance degrades systematically as context length grows. We diagnose this failure by decomposing performance into two factors--memory capture and memory retention--and quantitatively confirm that retention is the dominant bottleneck. Retention collapses because existing designs maintain memory as a monolithic text block, forcing every update to risk overwriting previously retained content. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose Multi-Head Recurrent Memory (MHM), a general, training-free framework that partitions memory into independent heads governed by a stage-wise select-then-update strategy. At each step, exactly one head is selected for update while the remaining heads are structurally shielded from overwriting, shifting the burden of retention from model behavior to architectural design. As a lightweight instantiation, we introduce Least-Recently-Updated MHM (MHM-LRU), which guarantees uniform head utilization with zero additional token overhead. Extensive experiments on long-context benchmarks show that MHM-LRU substantially improves both retention and end-to-end accuracy across the 100K--1M token range, where baselines degrade sharply. On RULER-HQA at 896K tokens, MHM-LRU improves the memory retention rate from less than 30% to 73.96%. These gains generalize across model families, scales, and task types, positioning architectural optimization as a practical and cost-efficient path toward reliable long-context recurrent memory.

</details>


### [69] [Wind-Aware Reinforcement Learning Control of a Small Quadrotor Using Learned Onboard Wind Estimation in Simulated Atmospheric Turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01528)
*Abdullah Al Tasim,Wei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段学习流水线，用于小型多旋翼飞行器在湍流边界层中的轨迹跟踪：第一阶段利用注意力增强的门控循环网络从机载运动学和动力学估计局部风向量；第二阶段将冻结的风估计器输出输入给近端策略优化（PPO）强化学习控制器。实验表明，风估计器在未见风况下达到 0.40 m/s 的均方根误差和 3.2° 的方向误差；PPO 控制器在 4–12 m/s 平均风速下将水平跟踪误差降低 48%，并在所有评估回合中胜出；消融研究显示风感知收益随风速增加而上升，强风下约占总收益的一半；控制器在分布外风速（13–15 m/s）下仍能优雅降级，而基线则灾难性失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型多旋翼飞行器越来越多地在大气边界层执行任务，其中与飞行器空速相当的湍流风会显著降低轨迹跟踪性能，甚至使常规反馈控制失效。现有方法缺乏对局部风场的实时感知，难以应对强湍流和风切变环境。

Method: 采用两阶段学习流水线：1) 风估计器：基于注意力增强的门控循环网络（GRU），在数千次模拟飞行中训练，输入为机载运动学和动力学数据，输出水平风向量，训练环境包含冯·卡门湍流、幂律风切变和风向偏转；2) 飞行控制器：使用近端策略优化（PPO）算法，接收冻结的风估计器输出作为观测的一部分，进行强化学习训练。基线为无风感知的比例-微分（PD）控制器。通过三向消融实验分解性能提升来源。

Result: 风估计器在未见风况下实现每飞行 0.40 m/s 的均方根误差和 3.2° 方向误差，接近未分辨湍流施加的理论下限；在垂直爬升剖面上相对于恒定风基准的技能得分为 0.861。PPO 控制器在 4–12 m/s 平均风速下将水平轨迹跟踪误差降低 48%，在 100% 评估回合中优于基线。消融分析显示：运动学成分（无风信息）提供基础收益，风感知成分随风速增加而上升，强风下约占总收益的一半，符合空气动力学阻力的二次方缩放规律。在分布外风速 13–15 m/s 下，控制器优雅降级，而基线灾难性失效。

Conclusion: 该两阶段学习流水线有效结合了风场估计与强化学习控制，显著提升了多旋翼飞行器在强湍流边界层中的轨迹跟踪鲁棒性。风估计器精度接近物理极限，PPO 控制器能充分利用风感知信息，且在分布外条件下表现出优雅降级特性，为实际部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Small multirotor aircraft are increasingly tasked with operations in the atmospheric boundary layer, where turbulent winds comparable to the vehicle's airspeed degrade trajectory tracking and can defeat conventional feedback control. This work illustrates a two-stage learning pipeline that first estimates the local wind from onboard kinematics and dynamics and then exploits that estimate inside a reinforcement learning (RL) flight controller. The wind estimator, an attention-augmented gated recurrent network trained on thousands of simulated flights through von Karman turbulence with power-law shear and veer, recovers the horizontal wind vector with a per-flight root-mean-square error of 0.40 m/s and a direction error of 3.2 degrees on unseen wind regimes, an accuracy near the floor imposed by unresolved turbulence, and generalizes to vertical ascent profiles with a skill score of 0.861 over a constant-wind reference. A proximal policy optimization controller receiving the frozen estimator's output reduces horizontal trajectory tracking error by 48% relative to a wind-blind proportional-derivative baseline across mean winds of 4 m/s to 12 m/s, winning on 100% of evaluation episodes. A three-way ablation decomposes this improvement into a kinematic component, available without wind information, and a wind-perception component; the perception share rises with wind speed, from small in light winds toward roughly half the total benefit in strong winds, consistent with the quadratic scaling of aerodynamic drag. The controller degrades gracefully on out-of-distribution winds of 13 m/s to 15 m/s, where the baseline fails catastrophically.

</details>


### [70] [Certified World Models as Sensing Clocks: Drift-Aware Deadlines for Active Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01537)
*Hongbo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于认证世界模型的感知时钟原语，将预测有效性时界转化为操作性感知调度规则，并开发了漂移感知的部署方法。在冻结的3D VN-JEPA模型和合成定理测试台上验证了该时钟的有效性，能显著减少尾部违规，但也指出了短时域下共形时界可达到类似性能，谱项无明显优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型缺乏对预测有效时长的认证估计，导致智能体无法可靠地决定何时停止惯性预测并重新感知。需要一种能提供部署保证的感知调度原语，同时考虑学习模型中的分布漂移问题。

Method: 从经过审计的等变世界模型出发，推导无感知区间的截止期限；提出漂移感知部署方法：仅使用流形上Lyapunov率会高估惯性有效性，而校准的原生展开漂移包络才能提供部署保证。在冻结的3D VN-JEPA模型和合成的提示条件定理测试台上进行实验验证。

Result: 在冻结3D VN-JEPA模型上，感知时钟能控制跨种子和数据分片的区间同时证书违规；在定理测试台上，时钟在部署分布上保持有效，并在相同感知预算下大幅减少事件尾部违规。但也发现短时域下经验共形时界在有效性和预算上匹配部署时钟，部分重置探索未发现谱项有明确的预算匹配优势。

Conclusion: 主要贡献是认证感知时钟原语和漂移感知部署方法，而非声称谱时钟在经验上支配所有非谱调度器。该工作为世界模型的可靠感知调度提供了理论基础和实用方法。

Abstract: Certified world models estimate how long their predictions remain valid. We turn this validity horizon into an operational sensing clock: a rule for when an agent should stop coasting and re-sense. Starting from an audited equivariant world model, we derive a deadline for no-sensing intervals and show that deployable deadlines in learned world models must be drift-aware: on-manifold Lyapunov rates alone overestimate coasting validity, while calibrated native rollout-drift envelopes carry the deployed guarantee. On a frozen 3D VN-JEPA model, the resulting clock controls held-out interval-simultaneous certificate violation across seeds and data shards. In a cue-conditioned theorem-bed (a synthetic bench where all schedulers share the exact model, isolating the scheduling rule), the clock remains valid on the deployment distribution and substantially reduces eventful-tail violations relative to exact-mixture expected-belief scheduling at matched sensing budget. We also report limits: in the short-horizon frozen VN-JEPA regime, empirical conformal horizons match the deployed clock on validity and budget, and a partial-reset exploration finds no clean budget-matched advantage for the spectral term. Thus the contribution is a certified sensing-clock primitive and drift-aware deployment method, not a claim that spectral clocks empirically dominate all non-spectral schedulers.

</details>


### [71] [Evolutionary Feature Engineering for Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01548)
*Ege Onur Taga,Yilin Zhuang,M. Emrullah Ildiz,Petros Mol,Abhimanyu Das,Karthik Duraisamy,Samet Oymak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出进化特征工程(EFE)框架,利用大语言模型驱动的进化算法自动发现结构化数据的预处理变换,在时间序列预测和表格预测两个任务上提升模型精度并保持可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型虽被用作进化优化中的开放式搜索算子,但缺乏针对结构化数据特征工程的系统性框架,且难以同时兼顾准确性与可解释性。

Method: EFE将变换表示为带有标准fit/transform接口的Python程序,可直接嵌入机器学习流水线;进化过程中利用数据集上下文、统计摘要及验证集下游性能反馈迭代优化候选程序。实例化为EFE-Time(时间序列可逆归一化)和EFE-Tab(表格紧凑特征程序)。

Result: EFE-Time在多个数据集上平均降低预测误差(MASE、WQL、MAE)3%以上,COVID-Deaths数据集高达19%,且对Chronos-2等最新时序基础模型有效。EFE-Tab生成的紧凑特征程序在决策树上达到竞争性精度并保持可解释性,优于或持平现有LLM特征工程方法。

Conclusion: EFE证明基于LLM的进化搜索能同时提升结构化数据任务的准确性与可解释性,为自动化特征工程提供了通用且有效的范式。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used as open-ended search operators in evolutionary optimization. We introduce Evolutionary Feature Engineering (EFE), a framework for using LLM-based evolution to discover preprocessing transformations for structured data. EFE represents transformations as Python programs with a standardized fit/transform interface, allowing them to be inserted directly into existing machine learning pipelines. During evolution, candidate programs are refined using dataset context, summary statistics, and downstream performance feedback on validation set. We instantiate EFE in two settings. For time-series forecasting, EFE-Time learns invertible, dataset-specific normalizations that improve off-the-shelf time-series foundation models. It reduces forecasting errors (MASE, WQL, MAE) 3% or more when averaged across datasets and improvements are as much as 19% on the COVID-Deaths dataset. Notably, these improvements occur with recent TSFMs such as Chronos-2. For tabular prediction, EFE-Tab evolves compact feature programs that add useful interpretable features and remove redundant ones, improving or matching existing LLM-based feature-engineering methods. We found EFE-Tab to be particularly effective on classical decision trees, where small sets of evolved features yield competitive accuracy while preserving interpretability. Overall, EFE demonstrates that LLM-based evolution can improve both accuracy and interpretability when automatically tackling structured data.

</details>


### [72] [X-LogSMask: Expand Transformer for Graph-Structured Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01553)
*Leyan Li,Rennong Yang,Zhenxing Zhang,Liping Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 X-LogSMask，一种可解释的多头对数结构掩码，将图拓扑直接注入 Transformer 注意力 logits，使自注意力在不改变架构的情况下成为有效的图学习算子。实验显示在 20 个基准上 13 个达到 SOTA，且单层轻量配置仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 Transformer 的全对全自注意力与图数据稀疏、结构化、多尺度的交互特性不匹配；现有 Graph Transformer 通过结构编码、混合消息传递或学习式注意力约束解决，但引入额外复杂度且可解释性有限。

Method: X-LogSMask 采用对数变换将对称归一化邻接矩阵的不同幂次分配给不同注意力头，直接注入注意力 logits，形成拓扑感知门控信号：抑制无拓扑支持的节点交互，保留特征依赖注意力；每头获得明确的结构半径，支持单层内多跳信息传播。并指出标准 Transformer 可视为完全图上的一步消息传递，X-LogSMask 即为拓扑约束的替代方案。

Result: 在 20 个节点、边、图级基准上，配备 X-LogSMask 的 Transformer 在 13 个数据集上达到最优性能，且在轻量单层配置下仍保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 简单、可解释的结构掩码即可使自注意力成为有效的图学习算子，无需修改 Transformer 架构。

Abstract: Transformers have become general-purpose architectures, but their all-to-all self-attention is poorly matched to graph data, whose interactions are sparse, structured and multi-scale. Existing Graph Transformers address this mismatch through structural encodings, hybrid message-passing modules or learned attention constraints, often introducing additional complexity and limited interpretability. Here we introduce X-LogSMask, an explainable multi-head logarithmic structural mask that injects symmetrically normalized graph topology directly into attention logits. The logarithmic transform converts structural connectivity into a topology-aware gating signal, suppressing unsupported node interactions while preserving feature-dependent attention. By assigning different powers of the normalized adjacency matrix to different attention heads, X-LogSMask gives each head a defined structural radius and supports multi-hop information propagation within a single layer. We further show that a standard Transformer encoder can be interpreted as one-step message passing on a complete graph, motivating X-LogSMask as a topology-constrained alternative to unrestricted self-attention. Across 20 node-, edge- and graph-level benchmarks, Transformers equipped with X-LogSMask achieve state-of-the-art performance on 13 datasets and remain competitive in a lightweight one-layer configuration. These results show that simple, interpretable structural masks can make self-attention an effective graph-learning operator without changing the Transformer architecture. The code is available at https://github.com/LiLeyan-0120/X-LogSMask.

</details>


### [73] [Geometric Signatures of Reasoning: A Spectral Perspective on Task Hardness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01571)
*Aria Masoomi,Mahsa Bazzaz,Adel Javanmard,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型中思维链推理轨迹在隐状态空间的几何特性，引入有效维度 d_ρ 量化轨迹复杂度，发现谱越平坦对应任务越难；动力学特征可在生成前 20% token 时预测答案正确性，并在不同难度问题间迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多关注思维链的长度与内容，而对其在隐状态空间中的内在几何结构知之甚少。作者旨在从几何视角形式化推理轨迹，揭示轨迹形状与任务难度、解答质量的关系，为早停策略提供理论依据。

Method: 将每条推理链建模为 ℝ^d 中的离散曲线，提取谱、位置、动力学三类几何泛函；定义有效维度 d_ρ 表征轨迹探索隐维度的广度；利用均值位置、位置离散度、初始/当前隐状态、均值速度、均值速率、速率离散度等动力学特征训练分类器预测最终正确性。

Result: 在 MATH500 数学推理数据集上，d_ρ 区分易/难问题的 AUC 达 0.93；动力学特征仅用前 20% 生成 token 即可预测正确性，且该预测能力跨难度迁移。

Conclusion: 推理轨迹的几何形状是模型内部推理过程的原则性窗口，有效维度可量化任务难度，动力学特征可实现早期正确性预测，为可解释性及高效推理提供新工具。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning enables large language models (LLMs) to solve complex problems by generating intermediate reasoning steps. While much attention has been paid to the length and content of these reasoning chains, far less is known about their internal geometry. We study the \emph{geometry} of CoT trajectories in the hidden state space of transformer models, formalizing each reasoning chain as a discrete curve in $\mathbb{R}^d$ and characterizing it through spectral, positional, and kinematic geometric functionals. We introduce the effective dimension $d_ρ$ as a measure of trajectory complexity and show theoretically that trajectories with flatter eigenvalue spectra correspond to harder tasks, as they explore more of the hidden dimensions. Lastly, we explore how kinematic features of the trajectory, mean position, positional dispersion, initial and current hidden states, mean velocity, mean speed, and speed dispersion, can be used to predict solution correctness before generation is complete, and may inform future early-stopping strategies. Experimentally, on mathematical reasoning problems from the MATH500 dataset, $d_ρ$ achieves $0.93$ AUC in distinguishing easy from hard problems, while kinematic features potentially can predict correctness from only the first $20\%$ of generated tokens. These correctness signatures transfer across questions of varying difficulty, establishing that the shape of a model's internal reasoning trajectory is a principled window into both task hardness and solution quality.

</details>


### [74] [BOUNDARY_SYNC: Measuring Communication-Induced Representational Coupling in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01600)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 BOUNDARY_SYNC 协议，利用耦合放大因子 (CAF) 量化大语言模型智能体间通信导致的表征耦合。实验发现：文本通信显著导致同质化 (CAF=0.803)；图像通信亦同质化；群体规模调节耦合方向 (K=5 同质化，K=3 多样化)；跨模型差异极大；耦合无状态，由提示上下文驱动，持续共识单调收敛。确立了 LLM 智能体耦合真实、可测、可在提示层面控制，对多智能体系统设计有直接启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型作为通信智能体被部署，研究者关注智能体间通信是否会导致输出趋同（同质化）或发散（多样化），并缺乏定量度量协议，因此需要一种可测量、可控的框架来刻画和调控多智能体系统中的表征耦合。

Method: 提出 BOUNDARY_SYNC 协议，定义耦合放大因子 CAF = JSD_cond / JSD_baseline (JSD 为 Jensen-Shannon 散度)，CAF<1 表示同质化，>1 表示多样化。在受控 GPT-4o 实验中 (N=30, ~9900 次 API 调用) 测量文本与图像通信下的耦合；进行无通信消融、提示扰动控制、群体规模 (K=3,5) 对比、跨模型复现 (含 DeepSeek)、无状态性验证 (提示上下文 vs 累积更新) 及持续共识单调收敛实验。

Result: 1) 文本通信显著同质化 (CAF=0.803 [0.740,0.873], d=1.30, p<0.001)，消融与扰动实验确认。2) 图像通信在模态内基线下亦同质化 (CAF=0.834 [0.811,0.858])，比例效应相当。3) 群体规模调节方向：K=5 同质化，K=3 产生 CAF>1 (点估计 1.14 与 1.06)，显示向多样化的方向性转移。4) 跨模型复现极大差异 (CAF 0.034–0.803)，DeepSeek 受格式伪影主导。5) 耦合无状态——由提示上下文驱动而非累积更新，持续共识产生单调收敛。

Conclusion: LLM 智能体耦合真实存在、可量度、可在提示层面控制。BOUNDARY_SYNC 与 CAF 为多智能体系统设计提供了诊断与调控工具，揭示通信模态、群体规模、模型异质性对耦合方向与强度的决定性影响，为构建可控、鲁棒的多智能体协作奠定实证基础。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are deployed as communicating agents, does inter-agent communication cause outputs to converge? We introduce BOUNDARY_SYNC, a protocol measuring representational coupling via the Coupling Amplification Factor (CAF = JSD_cond / JSD_baseline), where CAF < 1 indicates homogenization and CAF > 1 indicates diversification. In controlled GPT-4o experiments (N=30, ~9,900 API calls), we measure coupling in text and image communication. Key findings: (1) text communication causes significant homogenization (CAF=0.803 [0.740, 0.873], d=1.30, p<0.001), confirmed by no-communication ablation and prompt-perturbation controls; (2) image communication also homogenizes under within-modality baselines (CAF=0.834 [0.811, 0.858]), with comparable proportional effect; (3) group size moderates coupling direction -- K=5 produces homogenization while K=3 yields CAF > 1.0 (point estimates 1.14 and 1.06, CI pending), suggesting a directional shift toward diversification; (4) cross-model replication shows extreme variation (CAF 0.034-0.803), with DeepSeek dominated by format artifacts; (5) coupling is stateless -- driven by prompt context rather than cumulative updating, with continuous consensus producing monotonic convergence. These results establish LLM agent coupling as real, measurable, and controllable at the prompt level, with direct implications for multi-agent system design.

</details>


### [75] [SINA: A Fully Automated Circuit Schematic Image to Netlist Generator Using Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01609)
*Saoud Aldowaish,Yashwanth Karumanchi,Kai-Chen Chiang,Mohammed Ayman Habib,Finn Murphy,Rishen Cao,Morteza Fayazi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SINA是一个开源的电路原理图图像到网表生成器，通过深度学习、连通分量标记、OCR和视觉语言模型实现全自动化流程，支持IC和PCB级原理图，准确率96.67%，比现有方法高2.72倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI在模拟和混合信号电路设计中的应用受限于缺乏机器可读的电路设计知识表示；文献、教科书、网站中的原理图图像构成了大量验证过的设计资源，但无法直接被EDA工具处理，转换为网表对于仿真、验证和构建AI模型数据库至关重要。现有转换方法在IC和PCB级原理图间缺乏泛化能力，且在元件识别、连接推断、区分连接节点与交叉导线方面表现不佳。

Method: SINA提出全自动化流水线，集成深度学习进行鲁棒元件检测，连通分量标记进行准确连接推断，OCR提取元件参考标识符，视觉语言模型（VLM）进行可靠的参考标识符分配。支持IC和PCB级原理图，包含专门的交叉导线检测以区分导线交叉与连接。使用图同构技术验证生成网表的正确性。

Result: 实验显示整体网表生成准确率达到96.67%，比最先进方法高2.72倍。

Conclusion: SINA有效解决了电路原理图图像到网表转换的泛化性、元件识别、连接推断和交叉导线区分等难题，为模拟/混合信号电路设计知识的机器可读化提供了高精度开源工具，推动EDA领域AI应用发展。

Abstract: Recent advances in Artificial Intelligence (AI) have revolutionized Electronic Design Automation (EDA), particularly through Large Language Models (LLMs) for circuit design tasks. However, their application to analog and mixed-signal domains remains limited by the lack of machine-readable representations of existing circuit design knowledge. Circuit schematic images found in research manuscripts, textbooks, and websites constitute a vast repository of validated designs; however, these visual representations cannot be directly processed by EDA tools. Converting them into machine-readable netlists is essential for enabling simulation, verification, and building comprehensive databases for AI-based models. Current conversion methods lack generalization across both Integrated Circuit (IC) and Printed Circuit Board (PCB) level schematics. Moreover, they struggle with component recognition and connectivity inference, and fail to distinguish between connected junctions and crossing wires. In this paper, we propose SINA, an open-source circuit schematic image-to-netlist generator. SINA is a fully automated pipeline that integrates deep learning for robust component detection, connected-component labeling for accurate connectivity inference, Optical Character Recognition (OCR) for component reference designator extraction, and a Vision-Language Model (VLM) for reliable reference designator assignment. SINA handles both IC- and PCB-level schematics and incorporates dedicated crossing-wires detection to differentiate wire intersections from connections. We validate the correctness of the generated netlists using graph isomorphism techniques. Our experiments demonstrate an overall netlist generation accuracy of 96.67%, which is 2.72x higher compared to state-of-the-art approaches.

</details>


### [76] [MKGR: Multimodal Knowledge-Graph Representation Learning for Cold-Start Protein-Protein Interaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01627)
*Wenbo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG
